01 · The thesis

Most AI systems treat their layers as separate worlds: pixels live in shaders, embeddings live in HNSW, notes live in SQLite, claims live in a ledger, proofs live in Lean. Epistemos's thesis is that all of these are projections of one substrate. The substrate is addressable. The projections are typed. The relationship between them is a system of theorems, not a stack of glue code.

The mistake to avoid: collapsing the thesis into "everything is an EML tree." EML (Elementary Morph Language) is one primitive among many. The substrate fabric is wider — it includes Geometry-IR, Scan-IR, Info-IR, Operator-IR, Tropical-IR, raw vectors, raw pixels, graph topology, and residency tiers.

Canonical statement. Everything is one substrate object, expressed through different coordinates, planes, residencies, and primitive representations. Pixels, vectors, notes, graph nodes, KV pages, model components, AnswerPackets, tool results, and proofs are typed projections of the same addressable fabric. — UNIFIED_ACTIVE_SUBSTRATE_CANON_2026_05_16

01A · The lattice brain — every datum as a coordinate point

The real frame is not "a chatbot with memory." Epistemos is a living coordinate geometry: every note, pixel, vector, claim, proof, KV page, tool event, model output, and UI projection is lifted into a point in a typed lattice. A point is not just data. It carries address, plane, residency, primitive form, error budget, and witness. Intelligence is the system learning better geometry over that lattice.

That is where EML and the primitive IR stack matter. EML-IR is the elementary-function floor; it does not swallow the whole universe. Geometry-IR handles rotors, Clifford structure, phase, and spatial transport. Info-IR disciplines confidence through KL, Bregman geometry, and mirror descent. Scan-IR and Operator-IR capture recurrence, SSM state, time stepping, and PDE-like operators. Tropical-IR captures max-plus and piecewise-linear structure. The "brain" is the evolving composition lattice between these primitives. The LLM is one projector over that lattice: it proposes text, code, lowerings, and explanations, but it does not own the coordinates.

The unifying primitive is the substrate address, not the model and not a single IR syntax. A UasAddress is the coordinate handle that lets the same machinery point at a note block, graph neighborhood, vector page, KV page, adapter delta, MoE expert, SSM state, parameter anchor, proof obligation, or AnswerPacket witness. If the architecture lands, prompting becomes the least interesting path: the app speaks to the model's addressable "brain" by routing into the correct active coordinates, not by dumping a long prompt into a black box.

Target-state axisTechnical meaningEnd-state if it works
One address primitiveUasAddress + runtime plane + residency tier + WBO budget + witness pointer.Every object can be lifted, projected, mutated, replayed, and compared through one coordinate contract.
Minimal active computeActive Assembly wakes the smallest sufficient lattice slice: note atoms, graph islands, vector/KV pages, tool state, adapters, kernels.Storage and compute collapse toward compressed active subsets instead of whole-vault scans or giant prompt stuffing.
Model-brain addressingWhole model → output schema → KV page → weight layout → adapter delta → MoE expert → active assembly → SSM/head state → parameter anchor → circuit.The app stops merely calling a model and starts routing into typed cognitive components with explicit measurement boundaries.
Primitive geometryEML / Geometry / Info / Scan / Operator / Tropical IR provide lawful transforms between points."Understanding" becomes lower projection loss, better coordinates, and verified transforms, not just fluent text.
Self-improving substrateRun traces become supervised, preference, adapter-routing, and fast-memory deltas only after SCOPE-Rex/WBO/rollback gates.The system learns from use while preserving a stable base model and visible demotion path.
Coordinate fieldTarget payloadWhy it exists
UasAddress{ kind, payload_id, version, parent? }Stable identity across notes, graph nodes, KV pages, adapter deltas, tool outputs, proofs, and model-state handles.
RuntimePlaneState · Episodic · Assembly · Controller · VerificationPrevents UI, retrieval, action, and proof artifacts from pretending to be the same kind of authority.
ProductBuildMAS · ProNames which distributable build may expose the feature. Research and Vault are not builds.
ProStatus / ResidencyStatusLive · Gated · ResearchCandidate · VaultPreserved · Omega · CurrentApp · VerifiedFloor · CapabilityCeilingSeparates product availability, proof maturity, and runtime residency without changing identity.
PrimitiveKindRaw · Vector · EML · Geometry · Info · Scan · Operator · Tropical · ProofNames which geometry is allowed to transform the point.
ProjectionMapLift / Ingest · Project / Compress / Recall · Mutate / PromoteThere are only three legal motions; every route must declare which one it is performing.
LatticeBudgetWBO residual, compression loss, approximation class, drift deltaApproximation is not hidden. Compression, sketching, adapter promotion, and projection all pay an error account.
ModelAddressschema, KV page, adapter delta, MoE expert, SSM/head state, parameter anchor, circuitThe app can address smaller cognitive components instead of only making whole-model calls.
WitnessRefRunEventLog, AnswerPacket, ClaimGraph, falsifier artifact, Lean proof, rollback recordEvery meaningful state change can be inspected, replayed, rejected, or demoted.
LayerTheoretical roleWhy it matters
Typed latticeEvery artifact becomes a coordinate point with address, plane, residency, budget, primitive form, and witness.Notes, pixels, vectors, claims, proofs, and model state stop being separate worlds.
EML-IRElementary-function floor: exp, log, trig, powers, symbolic normal forms, and ULP-checkable kernels.The app gets a small arithmetic alphabet instead of trusting opaque text math.
Geometry-IRRotors, Clifford structure, phase, transport, and spatial reasoning.This is the "perfect geometry" lane: coordinate transforms become typed, testable, and composable.
Info / Scan / Operator / Tropical IRConfidence geometry, recurrence, PDE/operator lowering, and max-plus piecewise structure.The lattice can represent belief, time, dynamics, and neural-style piecewise computation.
Evolving substrateHyperdynamic schemas and repair loops mutate or promote primitive routes only with witnesses.The system learns by improving its geometry, not by letting a fluent answer become law.
LLM laneA projector that proposes text, code, explanations, and candidate lowerings over the active lattice region.The model stays useful without becoming memory, proof, authority, or the substrate itself.
Design rule. Do not say "the LLM remembers." The substrate remembers. Do not say "the LLM understands geometry." The primitive lattice owns geometry. A good run improves coordinates, reduces projection loss, pays WBO honestly, and leaves a witness.

01B · Clean nested architecture map — spine, organs, mechanisms, candidates

The architecture stops feeling chaotic when the names are kept at four levels. The spine is what happens in order. The organs own durable responsibilities. The mechanisms are implementation choices inside an organ. The candidates are preserved research ideas that cannot become product behavior until falsifiers and visible wiring prove them.

Namespace lock. Do not abbreviate Active Cold Storage as ACS. Use ColdStore or Cold Residency Layer. Keep AcsAnchor for the existing anchored coordinate/provenance object. Rename legacy ACS/Kuramoto language forward to KuramotoSync or ResonanceSync. Use SCOPE-Rex Admission, SovereignGate, or AdmissionGate for allow/warn/defer/quarantine/reject behavior.
Build lock. Epistemos has two distributable builds: MAS and Pro. MAS is the App Store-safe public floor. Pro is the direct-distribution power build. Research, Vault, Omega, heavy runtime, future substrate work, and speculative theorems are internal Pro status bands, not separate app builds.
North-star sentence. Epistemos is a local cognitive substrate where every meaningful object has an address, plane, budget, status, and witness; MAS ships the safe floor, Pro contains the gated/research/vault/omega ladder, and no claim promotes without visible proof.

Nested tree view

Epistemos ├─ Builds │ ├─ MAS Build │ │ └─ public safe floor · local-first · witnessed · App Store-safe │ └─ Pro Build │ ├─ Pro Live │ ├─ Pro Gated │ ├─ Pro Research │ ├─ Pro Vault-Preserved │ └─ Pro Omega ├─ Motions │ ├─ Lift / Ingest │ ├─ Project / Compress / Recall │ └─ Mutate / Promote ├─ Runtime planes │ ├─ State │ ├─ Episodic │ ├─ Assembly │ ├─ Controller │ └─ Verification ├─ Identity and meaning │ ├─ UAS: address / identity fabric │ ├─ OAS: semantic state over addresses │ └─ AcsAnchor: anchored coordinate / provenance object ├─ Residency and awakening │ ├─ ColdStore: dormant notes, vectors, KV pages, weights, adapters, graph islands │ ├─ ResidencyGovernor: leases, copy-count, mmap safety, eviction, quarantine │ └─ ActiveAssembly: minimal waking support set ├─ Evidence and truth │ ├─ VaultRecall / Halo / Shadow candidates │ ├─ Eidos pre-validation and closed citation universe │ ├─ Eidos post-validation against output and tool claims │ ├─ ClaimGraph / ClaimKind / VRM labels │ └─ SCOPE-Rex validity checks ├─ Governance and execution │ ├─ SovereignGate permissions │ ├─ RuntimeRouter / RouteProfile │ ├─ System G / AgentRuntime │ └─ Executors: local model, MLX, Apple Intelligence, tool, kernel, explicit provider ├─ Accounting and projection │ ├─ WBO / LatticeBudget │ ├─ ShadowProjection │ ├─ Primitive IR stack: EML, Geometry, Info, Scan, Operator, Tropical │ └─ Falsifier / Lean / benchmark witnesses └─ Visible proof ├─ RunEventLog ├─ MutationEnvelope ├─ AnswerPacket ├─ Provenance Console / Substrate Health └─ rollback / replay handle

The mission spine

PassClean verbOrganWhat it owns
1IntentCognitivePacket / MissionPacketTask class, allowed claim kinds, privacy, budget, deadline, output schema, and tool scope.
2AddressOAS / UASWhat exists, what it means, where it lives, and which stable address names it.
3AwakenColdStore / ResidencyGovernorDormant notes, vector pages, KV pages, adapters, model byte ranges, and graph islands surface as candidates without waking everything.
4AssembleActiveAssemblyThe minimal waking set: evidence, graph neighborhood, tools, model state, kernels, adapters, and runtime support.

6GovernSCOPE-Rex / SovereignGateMission, route, provider, cloud, file, shell, tool, memory, and mutation policy. This wraps the whole run, not only the end.
7ExecuteRuntimeRouter / Executor / System GLocal MLX, Apple Intelligence, Rust tool, Metal kernel, deterministic rule, or explicit gated provider route.
8VerifyEidos post-validation + SCOPE-RexDid the output cite real evidence? Did the tool result support the claim? Is any mutation still admitted?
9WitnessRunEventLog / AnswerPacketVisible answer, route, evidence, uncertainty, provider, denied actions, accepted writes, rollback, and replay handle.

Eidos → NeuralImportance bridge

The search organ can also seed the model-state route. Eidos still proves evidence first: hits, snippets, citation handles, why_matched, contradiction hints, and domain tags. Those fields can form an EidosRoutePrior that asks the NeuralImportanceAtlas which KV pages, adapter slices, weight-page families, kernels, verifier tools, or helper-controller state should wake. This is a route prior, not authority. UAS/AppColdStore binds addresses and bytes; ActiveAssembly picks the support set; SCOPE-Rex/SovereignGate admits it; RunEventLog and AnswerPacket show it.

Continuation prompt. Future full-architecture sessions should start from docs/audits/FULL_ARCHITECTURE_CONTINUATION_PROMPT_2026_05_31.md. It preserves the ambition that AppColdStore can hold a much larger addressable atlas than hot RAM while keeping the rigor lock: UAS makes cold bytes routable, not RAM-latency, and every promotion needs route cards, falsifiers, and visible proof.
Current hardening prompt. For new main-agent sessions, use docs/audits/SOVEREIGN_ARCHITECTURE_HARDENING_PROMPT_2026_06_06.md as the paste-ready successor, then read the full continuation prompt above. It preserves the owner's recursive architecture-hardening intent while adding L1/L2/L3 reporting, source-guard build order, commit/push checkpoints, and the June 6 TurboVec/QAT runtime-plural intake. This is a prompt/canon fold, not a product runtime promotion.

Formal math company intake

The Axiom / Axiomatic AI / UlamAI / Harmonic / Math Inc wave sharpens the same loop: ProblemCard → ConstructionGraph → verifier route → repair/golf → AnswerPacket. AXLE, Axplorer, AxiomProver, Ax-Prover, OProver, UlamAI, Aristotle, Gauss, OpenGauss, LeanSearch, Pantograph, and lean4-skills are source handles and adapters under existing organs, not new authorities. Source code is mined for motifs until license/setup/vendor gates pass. Extraordinary math claims need Lean/kernel artifacts, human-readable explanation, replay, rollback, and visible uncertainty.

Formal-math source. The agent-facing intake lives at docs/fusion/FORMAL_MATH_COMPANY_AND_LEAN_INTAKE_2026_06_01.md. It keeps the ambition alive while requiring route cards, falsifiers, and AnswerPacket proof before promotion.

Meta-breakthrough control surfaces

The next architecture lock is small cumulative control. A tiny proof card, premise card, feature handle, KV page policy, adapter decision, or brain route can be a real breakthrough when it is addressable, measured, and reversible. Epistemos can pursue neural control, but the product definition is strict: address, observe, select, intervene, measure, and roll back a bounded model-state unit. Anything less is a route prior, not a control claim.

Constructive residency paradigm

The 70B cocktail now has a stricter interpretation: do not imagine a dense resident model squeezed into hot memory. Construct the smallest proof-carrying resident assembly that can solve the task. UAS names cold bytes, AppColdStore packs them, Eidos shapes the task, the ResidencyConstructionGraph searches candidate support sets, ColdAssemblyPlan admits the selected hot/warm/cold tiles, and ProofCarryingResidencyLease makes every cold wake visible, budgeted, expiring, and reversible.

Residency PatternBoost adds the discovery loop above that runtime path: generate many UAS assembly genomes, repair invalid ones, sparsely fingerprint them, keep held-out winners, and distill the elite motifs into small route/layout priors before live execution. This is the Axplorer/PatternBoost lesson applied to cold residency itself, with Lattice Deduction Transformer-style abstention for wake, retrieve, continue, pause, resume, verify, or escalate decisions.

Cache-lineage autoresearch paradigm

The next storage upgrade is not hidden cache magic. KV/prefix units, prompt caches, runtime traces, browser traces, and route outcomes become UAS-addressed substrate with lineage, compatibility fences, privacy class, purge policy, admission cards, rollback, and AnswerPacket visibility. Constructive residency builds the current resident assembly; cache-lineage autoresearch remembers which prefixes, traces, and policies made future assemblies cheaper and better.

PrimitiveWhat it makes concretePromotion gate
KVPrefixUnitReusable prefix/KV chunk with model, tokenizer, adapter, prompt digest, token range, codec, privacy, and purge metadata.F-KVPrefixUnit-Lineage
KVCompatibilityFenceRejects stale or incompatible cache state before restore.F-KVCompatibilityFence
CacheAdmissionCardDecides persist, compress, evict, or purge under reuse, privacy, storage-wear, and rollback budgets.F-CacheAdmissionCard
ExecutionTraceCapsuleReplayable app/browser/runtime trace with redaction, artifacts, and failure signatures.F-ExecutionTraceCapsule
ParetoResidencyTournamentBounded search over prompt, cache, layout, quant, prefetch, and route policies.F-ParetoResidencyTournament
CacheMutationPatchReversible candidate policy/layout/prompt mutation with baseline, patch, ablation, held-out result, and rollback.F-CacheMutationPatch-Rollback
Cache-lineage source. The agent-facing intake lives at docs/fusion/CACHE_LINEAGE_AUTORESEARCH_PARADIGM_2026_06_01.md. Cached KV is not proof, and traces are not truth; they are reusable state only after compatibility, redaction, rollback, and falsifier gates.

Semantic working-set compiler

The newest plausibility bridge treats UAS/AppColdStore as a compiler and page-table problem. The app does not need the whole cold atlas hot. It needs a predicted, budgeted, prefetchable, observable working set for this mission: evidence pages, KV pages, adapter slices, model byte ranges, kernels, proof tools, and verifier lanes. A cold miss is a failed prediction, not just an I/O event; it must update the next layout, route, cache, or source policy.

Substrate trace observatory

The newest witness layer turns LLM visualizations, mechanistic probes, KV-cache pressure, source-grounded research, and agent action logs into a replayable microscope over the actual substrate route. Epistemos should not merely say a page, cache, model route, source, verifier, or tool mattered; it should emit a trace frame showing what woke, why it woke, what it cost, whether it helped, what failed, and what patch should be considered next.

Math and portable note systems

The note/editor research lane contributes the math that lets Epistemos stay fast and portable: editor transactions compose, projections bind source digests, CRDT merges need conflict witnesses, syntax trees update by changed ranges, graph/search/review views update by delta, and model-authored mutations are parsed while decoding. Tolaria and other Tauri apps are source motifs for files-first and Git-first workflows; they do not replace the native macOS shell.

Engineering logic architecture

The newest architecture layer is not another runtime organ. It is the logic grammar that lets the runtime stay ambitious without becoming vague: every mechanism gets a decision record, invariant, state machine or boundary contract, budget, failure envelope, observability probe, rollback, and falsifier before it claims live authority. Future agents should assume the target can be made real, then force the mechanism through explicit proof artifacts.

Where Helios lands

Helios is not a tenth spine step. It is the substrate-runtime research lineage underneath the organs: memory hierarchy, WBO/lattice doctrine, kernels, SCOPE-Rex lineage, model tracks, and scanner language. Product docs should translate "Helios" into the concrete organ being used: ColdStore for residency, WBO for error accounting, SCOPE-Rex for verification, RuntimeRouter for execution, Eidos for evidence, or Metal kernels for measured compute.

If a doc says...Translate to...Product rule
Helios memory hierarchyColdStore / ResidencyGovernor / WBOOnly claim live behavior after dry-run and crash-safe falsifiers pass.
Helios kernelsRuntimeRouter / Metal / primitive IR kernelsTarget-only until CPU reference, Metal witness, and product caller chain exist.
Helios theorem or WBO resultLatticeBudget / ClaimLedger / falsifier artifactKeep theorem state visible: proven, empirical, engineering bet, candidate, or blocked.
Helios model brainAddressable neural substrate / Parameter Connectome / ActiveAssemblyDo not claim arbitrary neuron control; claim addressed components only when implemented.

02 · Layman explanation

PLAIN
A normal AI app is a pile of features that look related but don't actually know about each other. The chat doesn't know what the search did; the search doesn't know what the agent decided; the agent doesn't know what the editor edited. Epistemos forces them all to share one filing system — every note, every model output, every tool result, every search hit gets the same kind of ID — so the app can reason about itself, undo itself, and explain itself.
PLAIN
It also keeps an honest score. Every time the system approximates something (compresses a vector, fuses two searches, picks a model), the cost gets written down. Every time it shows you an answer, it shows you which notes that answer came from. If it makes up a citation, the system rejects the answer itself, not you.
PLAIN
And it runs locally. Any provider route is explicit, user-scoped, and visible; your notes don't leave unless you ask them to.

03 · The nine-layer spine

03A · Active Assembly — the waking set

Active Assembly is the layer that makes the substrate feel brain-like without overclaiming direct neuron control. It is the rule that only the relevant support wakes: notes, graph neighborhoods, vault traces, vector pages, KV pages, tools, runtime state, and primitive witnesses are selected into one ActiveAssemblyPacket before inference or action.

This is the correct technical interpretation of the "best neuron group" metaphor today: Epistemos does not yet steer arbitrary internal model neurons live. It builds the addressable support-selection layer that makes future circuit routing, KV residency routing, kernel selection, and adapter activation possible. The "neuron" is currently a typed support object: a model component, KV page, vector region, graph island, or retrieved evidence packet with a UAS address and WBO budget.

Active Assembly componentFunctionCurrent status
research::active_assemblyPacket + selector substrate for support selection.substrate present · runtime consumer pending
research::page_gatherSketch top-k → residual rescore → escalation policy.CPU twin / falsifier work pending
W-04 / W-05Wire PageGather into vault retrieval and Active Assembly into agent runtime.Phase 2
F-ActiveAssembly-MinimalProves the selector wakes only relevant packets within budget.pending measured artifact

03B · Auto-routing — from intent to substrate lane

The routing layer is where user intent becomes a substrate path. It chooses not just "which model" but which evidence set, which runtime, which memory tier, which admission policy, and which witness obligations apply.

This is where the "auto-routing neuron" idea lands in rigorous terms. A future Parameter Connectome can expose model components as addressable objects, but the present substrate already defines the routing shape: feature/circuit candidates must become UAS-addressed objects; model selection must produce a RouteProfile; each route must declare a memory tier and WBO budget; and every escalation must leave a witness.

CURRENT
Model routing exists, but several RouteProfile tables are still placeholder or partial. Local MLX can run, but the full ActiveAssembly → ColdStore/ResidencyGovernor → SCOPE-Rex/SovereignGate → AnswerPacket route is not yet the default live path.
FUTURE
Parameter Connectome work (PCF-1..10) converts features, heads, low-rank components, adapters, and circuit probes into addressable substrate objects. That is the route from "best support set" to true circuit-adjacent routing.

03C · Evolving kernel — L_SE without destabilizing the base

The self-evolving kernel is not "the app rewrites itself whenever it feels like it." It is a quarantined adaptation lane. Surprise, drift, or repeated failure can propose a small adapter delta, but SCOPE-Rex/SovereignGate must admit it, WBO must budget it, and the witness layer must be able to replay or roll it back.

Kernel conceptMeaning in the architecturePromotion gate
L_SE / self-evolving tierAdapter or fast-weight lane that can learn from surprise without modifying base truth silently.T26 research-deferred
Autogenous Kernel Identity (E7)Doctrine target for a self-generating kernel under duplex + error-enriched convergence assumptions.theoretical
ControllerKernelPackTarget Metal/kernel bundle for controller primitives.F-ControllerKernelPack pending
WBO drift ledgerEvery adaptation delta must declare and measure drift.OpLog accounting live · drift artifact pending

03D · HELIOS V6.2 — future / Pro status gates

The Pro ceiling work is not a junk drawer. It is the status ladder: kernels and falsifiers that become app features only after local M2 Pro evidence exists. The current document keeps them in the architecture so they are not lost, but it marks them as target-only until measured.

No-compromise inclusion rule. MAS work builds the honest floor. Pro preserves high-power tools, direct-distribution capability, research candidates, vault-preserved ambition, and omega substrate work. Nothing is deleted because it is not shippable today. It is classified as MAS, Pro Live, Pro Gated, Pro Research, Pro Vault-Preserved, or Pro Omega with a witness and promotion gate.

03E · Continual learning — from use traces to adapters

The self-learning path is a governed online-adaptation pipeline, not a magical "the model improves itself" claim. Epistemos records every interaction as typed supervision: prompt context, active lattice coordinates, retrieved evidence, tool calls, accepted edits, rejected edits, verifier failures, latency, WBO residuals, and final AnswerPacket status. Those traces can be distilled into SFT examples, preference pairs, KTO/DPO-style corrections, adapter-routing labels, and hard negative retrieval examples.

The base checkpoint remains immutable. Learning attaches versioned deltas: LoRA / QLoRA rank adapters, DoRA magnitude-direction adapters, KTO or DPO preference heads, MoLoRA / AdaFuse expert routes, retrieval re-rankers, and eventually L_SE or Titans-MAC fast-memory modules. Each delta is a substrate object with a UAS address, parent checkpoint hash, training-set hash, rank/quantization metadata, evaluation card, drift ledger entry, rollback pointer, and SCOPE-Rex/SovereignGate admission verdict.

In the target architecture, using the app becomes a local data engine. Good behavior creates positive examples; corrections create contrastive pairs; failed citations create retrieval negatives; tool failures create repair obligations; repeated domain work creates adapter specialization. Promotion is never silent. A candidate adapter must beat the frozen baseline on local evals, preserve canary behavior, stay within WBO drift limits, and remain demotable by versioned registry rollback.

Learning stageTechnical mechanismApp feature it becomesStatus boundary
Trace captureRunEventLog, Active Assembly packet, retrieval support set, tool result, verifier verdict, AnswerPacket outcome.Every run becomes a typed training/eval record instead of a disposable chat transcript.event surfaces live · learning projection incomplete
Example distillationODIA-style filtering, deduplication, hard-negative mining, accepted-edit extraction, rejected-claim pairing.Idle scheduler builds local SFT, preference, retrieval, and repair datasets.scheduler/data generators present · adapter output pending

Preservation rule. Learning work is not deleted because it is hard to ship in MAS. MAS keeps the honest type surface and refusal states. Pro keeps Python/MLX training, adapter export/import, and MoLoRA inference. Pro Research keeps L_SE and Titans-style online adaptation until the falsifiers prove it can promote.

03F · Hyperdynamic schema — recursive repair until admissible

The "rerun a couple passes until it is perfect" idea is not a prompt trick. It is a typed repair loop: propose, check, repair, re-check, and only then emit or escalate. The loop can run over schemas, plans, citations, math derivations, tool calls, or agent actions, but it never gets to hide failed attempts.

Community terms for this layer are verifier-guided decoding, self-refinement, compiler-style lowering, repair loops, constrained generation, and proof-carrying execution. Epistemos adds the substrate discipline: every repair pass carries the same UAS address family, writes its attempts into RunEventLog, pays WBO if it approximates, and emits an AnswerPacket only when the relevant floor passes.

Repair domainVerifier floorVisible outputStatus
Closed-citation answersEidos / VaultRecall trace must cite real vault evidence.AnswerPacket citation badges in chat.Phase 2 W-46.1 / W-21.1

03G · Math lab — self-proving expressions, not just calculation

The in-app math idea should become a proof workbench: write an expression, derivation, physical system, optimization problem, or claim; lower it into the primitive IR stack; evaluate it locally; and attach either a certificate, a falsifier record, or an explicit proof obligation.

EML is crucial here, but not because every kind of data literally reduces to EML. EML is the elementary-function primitive. It covers the Liouvillian / elementary-expression lane: exp/log compositions, normal forms, symbolic regression over elementary functions, and numeric kernels that can be checked by F-ULP. Geometry-IR, Operator-IR, Scan-IR, Info-IR, and Tropical-IR cover the sibling domains that EML should not falsely claim.

Math featurePrimitive / theorem sourceWhat the user seesStatus
EML expression loweringT-EML-IR-Lowering · T-EML-Normal-FormExpression inspector with EML tree, normal form, numerical witness.T5/T12 substrate · product surface pending
Action-to-EML demoT-Action-to-EML · harmonic oscillator / pendulumPhysics equation lowers to EML + Lean-checked conservation claim.killer-demo target
Lean schema authorityT-Lean-Schema-Authority · ClaimLedgerRust/Swift/Lean schema round-trip with proof obligation badges.lake build green · eml-lean vendor missing
Numeric falsifier benchF-ULP-Oracle · Metal half-softmax · EML kernelsMeasured ULP panel: passed, failed, or empirical-only.Terminal F / M2 Pro run-first

Anti-drift rule. Any future audit that finds a research doc family without a row in this atlas must add one before coding. If the feature is not buildable now, it gets a tier, a falsifier, and a re-promotion codeword. It does not disappear.

03I · AI research construction engine — the Erdős unlock

The OpenAI unit-distance result matters to Epistemos because it is a clean demonstration of the exact loop this app should make local: a model searched for a construction, imported machinery from a distant field, produced a proof candidate, and the result survived expert verification. The architectural lesson is not "ask a chatbot harder questions." It is: turn research itself into a typed, testable, local substrate workflow.

In the Erdős problem, the old intuition was geometric: square-grid-like constructions looked optimal. The new construction crossed into algebraic number theory and beat the obvious coordinate system. That is the performance moral for Epistemos: a unified cognitive substrate should not merely store everything in one place; it should search for the coordinate chart, kernel, route, memory tier, adapter, or proof representation that makes the same object cheaper, faster, and more verifiable.

Obvious wins

These are the low-ambiguity upgrades that fall straight out of the thesis. If all data is one typed substrate, then performance improves whenever the app stops translating between worlds and starts choosing the cheapest valid projection of the same object.

Obvious performance winWhy the Erdős result points at itEpistemos featureVerifier
Better coordinate chartsThe result beat the square-grid intuition by changing mathematical coordinates.Coordinate Atlas Inspector: switch one object between pixel, vector, graph, EML, geometry, and proof charts.F-UAS-CopyCount + chart round-trip tests
Smaller active contextGood constructions wake the right structure, not the whole space.Active Assembly support minimizer before chat / agent / proof work.F-ActiveAssembly-Minimal
Fused primitive kernelsA proof construction compresses many local moves into one global move.Primitive IR compiler that lowers repeated EML / Scan / Geometry patterns into Metal kernels.F-ULP + kernel parity benches
Zero-copy route searchIf the same data has many charts, the best chart is the one that avoids conversion.RouteProfile optimizer: choose the Swift/Rust/Metal/MLX path with fewest copies and lowest witness cost.copy-count tracer
Adapter specializationInstead of making the base model do everything, discover small deltas that solve a local family.QLoRA / DoRA / MoLoRA adapter search, promoted only after drift and rollback checks.WBO drift + rollback falsifier
Quantization and compression searchOpenAI's Parameter Golf shows constrained search exposes strong compression ideas.Local Parameter Golf mode for Epistemos kernels, adapters, tokenizers, and KV-cache layouts.leaderboard + reproducible bench bundle
Counterexample-driven hardeningThe breakthrough was a counterexample to a trusted conjecture.Architecture Counterexample Engine: attempts to break E/T/W claims before they ship.falsifier suite
Proof-sized outputA useful discovery is not a long essay; it is the minimal construction humans/tools can check.AnswerPacket compression: claim, construction, sources, proof/falsifier, rollback path.AnswerPacket live · citations pending

Performance synthesis

The practical win is not that the app becomes faster by chanting "lattice." The win is that one object can be re-expressed in whichever coordinate chart makes the next operation cheapest: a pixel tile when rendering, a vector when searching, an EML term when proving, a graph neighborhood when recalling, a KV page when decoding, a Metal buffer when executing. The unified substrate gives the system a common identity for all of those views, so optimization becomes route selection rather than lossy translation.

OptimizationWhat changesWhy it mattersFloor test
Copy-count collapsePixel, tensor, note span, graph node, and proof witness point to the same UAS object instead of separate duplicated payloads.Less memory pressure, fewer Swift↔Rust↔Metal↔MLX transfers, cleaner provenance.F-UAS-CopyCount
Active support minimizationActive Assembly wakes the smallest sufficient support set instead of passing the whole vault or whole graph to the model.Lower prompt cost, faster local inference, better citation discipline.F-ActiveAssembly-Minimal
Kernel fusionRepeated EML / Scan / Geometry patterns become one specialized Metal or Rust kernel when the proof and CPU reference agree.Hot loops stop paying interpreter and allocation overhead.F-ULP + kernel parity
RouteProfile optimizationThe route chooser compares deterministic, embedding, small-model, mid-model, cloud, and proof routes as projections of one request.The app can pick the cheapest route that still satisfies witness and capability gates.routeProfiles() production table
Adapter localityLearning becomes small local deltas attached to object families, not blind mutation of the base model.Supports "auto fine-tune as you use it" without corrupting the global model.WBO drift + rollback ledger
Shadow-near retrievalRecall can use higher-dimensional closeness even when surface text is far apart.Finds structural analogies, not just keyword overlap.F-Erdos-Lift-Optimality
Proof-sized answersAnswerPacket returns claim, support, construction, falsifier/proof status, and rollback path.Less rambling output; more checkable knowledge.AnswerPacket live · citation badges pending
Negative evidence reuseFailed constructions are stored as coordinates in the ConstructionGraph.The system learns where not to search next, which is a real performance gain for research loops.promotion ledger

Architecture simplification. The no-compromise V2/V3 loop becomes: ProblemCard → ConstructionGraph → Falsifier/Lean witness → AnswerPacket → promotion or fork. That one loop covers math, kernels, local inference, UI architecture, adapter learning, graph retrieval, and future research. Performance is not an afterthought; the winning construction is the one with the best verified speed, memory, copy-count, error budget, and user-visible proof.

03J · Performance dual — Erdős + Parameter Golf

The simplification is one invariant, not fewer ambitions. Epistemos treats every note, pixel, vector, graph node, KV page, model component, EML term, proof, and chat answer as one substrate object that can move in three ways: lift into the substrate, project out of the substrate, or mutate inside the substrate under witness.

Builder source. The agent-facing version lives at docs/fusion/SHADOW_PROJECTION_AND_RESEARCH_CONSTRUCTION_2026_05_24.md. It is state: candidate: no L8/E8/E9/ShadowProjection promotion without local falsifiers and WRV production caller chains.

The Substrate Motion Invariant

Lift / Ingestsurface → substrateRaw material becomes an addressable substrate object.note bytes · pixels · prompt · model output · local traceUAS address · source hash · plane
Project / Compress / Recallsubstrate → surfaceThe object becomes cheaper, smaller, visible, or usable in a specific chart.vault hit · Eidos citation · graph neighborhood · MLX context · Metal buffer · EML termShadowProjection · WBO budget · citation/proof
Mutate / Promotesubstrate → substrateDurable state changes, learns, adapts, or promotes into stronger authority.adapter update · governed tool action · kernel promotion · falsifier PASS · Lean certificateMutationEnvelope · SCOPE-Rex/SovereignGate verdict · rollback

03K · Addressable neural substrate — the large local model endgame

The original no-compromise target is stronger than RAG, MoE, or subquadratic attention. Epistemos tries to make the model's internal computation addressable: a dense model remains the full brain, but the runtime wakes only the verified active assembly of layers, components, KV pages, adapters, residual islands, and kernels needed for the current pass.

Builder source. This target is now indexed at docs/fusion/ADDRESSABLE_NEURAL_SUBSTRATE_CANON_2026_05_24.md. It is canon as the architecture and vocabulary, not as a shipped capability. Production requires the falsifier gates below.

What the app calls instead of "just the LLM"

NeuralSubstrateAddressSetaddressed layer blocks, rank-one components, KV pages, adapters, residual islands, activation masksState / Assembly
ResidencyPlanwhat is hot in RAM, compressed in RAM, cold on SSD, or escalated to networkEpisodic
KernelRouteMLX, Metal, Rust, Scan-IR, EML kernel, ternary GEMM, dense fallbackController

Capability gates

GateClaim it protects
F-Sparse-Runtime-Splitselected neural assembly reproduces dense execution within bounded drift

Precise claim. Epistemos is not literally poking arbitrary neurons today. The canon target is addressable neural substrate routing: layers, rank-one components, KV pages, adapters, residual islands, activation masks, and kernels become UAS objects. The SSM/router calls the right active assembly, and the dense/reference path checks whether that call was correct.

03L · Online research intake — public work becomes local doctrine only through falsifiers

The no-compromise rule is not to ignore public research. It is to absorb it without losing the architecture's spine. External papers, GitHub records, forks, PRs, and forum discoveries enter Epistemos as motifs first, then become W/P/T/F work only after they are mapped to UAS, ColdStore or AcsAnchor when relevant, WBO, admission, witnesses, build/status, and rollback.

Builder source. The operational intake protocol lives at docs/fusion/ONLINE_RESEARCH_INTAKE_SHADOW_PROJECTION_2026_05_24.md. Its core chain is source → motif → local primitive → W/P/T/F row → tier → falsifier → rollback → implementation.

Verifier-calibrated sparse route compiler

The Axiom/Axplorer lesson is not merely "use a model for math." It is generate, repair, score, keep winners, and repeat under a verifier. The Axiomatic/OProver lesson adds proof pressure: compiler errors, missing premises, failed-attempt memory, and repair trajectories are useful route labels. Epistemos applies both patterns to route selection itself: a tiny always-hot TwoStageRouteScout proposes a SparseWakeProposal, a BudgetedUncertaintyEscalator abstains when calibration is weak, a VerifierBudgetAuction rejects over-budget or low-evidence wakes, QueryAwareKVSelector and LayerKVJointLease pick likely-critical pages and depth together, FastWeightQuarantine records bounded scout deltas as shadow evidence, DepthLeaseCheckpoint forces shallow/deeper/full-depth choices to carry fallback, resume, rollback, RunEventLog, and AnswerPacket proof before dynamic depth can promote, ShadowWakeOracle converts full-wake/proof/test traces into visible non-authoritative labels, AblationShadowRun proves claimed useful units survive counterfactual removal before route-importance claims promote, AxiomAxiomaticSourceDistinction keeps axioms, retrieved sources, oracle labels, verifier traces, and route priors source-distinct before source-derived route claims can promote, SparseRouteNoHiddenAuthority proves those source/proof/oracle/PatternBoost/fast-weight/scout/sparse-wake signals cannot become hidden live route authority, ColdStreamNoHiddenAuthority proves transport cannot wake bytes or mutate route policy without SemanticWorkingSetPlan, ResidencyPageTable, SCOPE-Rex/SovereignGate admission, rollback, RunEventLog, and AnswerPacket proof, LargeModelProviderReferenceDeferral proves default MLX routing defers provider/fp16, KV-Direct 128K, dense 70B, and live sparse 70B work without provider calls or runtime bytes, ProviderRouteCopySourceGuard proves the Living Index and lattice copy cannot source-launder those lanes into product or runtime claims, TransportTraceAnswerPacket proves cold-transport-shaped answers expose byte movement, stalls, copies, fallback, rollback, and trace caveats in visible AnswerPacket surfaces, SSDWearBudget proves repeated transport plans expose wear, energy, cache, and write-amplification budgets before SSD-backed route claims can promote, ColdStreamVsMmap proves same-fixture mmap-fault, naive pread, and ColdStream benchmark-plan comparisons remain source-grounded, rollback-bound, visible, and metadata-only before live transport benchmarks, SlabArenaCopyCount proves CPU slabs preallocate and report copies/allocation samples before Metal I/O can promote, stale-slab rejection and repair instead of silent token blocking, ProductRouteReview proves red product routes stay red before runtime harness planning, SmallModelRuntimeHarnessSafetyPlan proves the small-model runtime harness safety plan is serialized, owner-gated, dry-run-first, cancellable, rollback-bound, AnswerPacket-visible, privacy-fenced, subprocess-free, and zero-runtime-byte before a dry-run witness, SmallModelRuntimeHarnessDryRunWitness proves the small-model runtime harness dry-run witness can replay runtime-shaped evidence without loading model bytes, mutating route policy, or cancellation, rollback, RunEventLog, AnswerPacket, privacy, budget, and execution-deferred proof before any live model bytes load, SmallModelRuntimeHarnessAbortableRuntimeProbe proves those lanes can be aborted before runtime/model bytes open with deadline, rollback, RunEventLog, and AnswerPacket evidence, SmallModelRuntimeHarnessLoggedRuntimeSmoke proves the runtime harness logs missing local hand off to retained proof without fresh product runtime/model bytes, SmallModelRuntimeHarnessProductRouteCapabilityRecheck turns that handoff into visible blocker cards instead of route authority, with zero new byte loads, and SmallModelRuntimeHarnessFreshProductRuntimeL3CapabilityCloseoutProbe closes that proof as red blocker evidence, new bytes, SmallModelRuntimeHarnessFreshProductRuntimeL3ReleaseAuditAutomatedChecksProbe records a RED automated-checks ledger with build, graph-engine, omega-mcp, and omega-ax passing while evidence without runtime OSLog, manual verification, distribution/compliance, or release-green claims. The consumed cursor chain remains parked at small_model_runtime_harness_fresh_product_runtime_l3_release_audit_automated_checks_probe; L2 remains vault_research_route_with_packetized_mitigation with that same next bottleneck, and broader product capability and release readiness remain unchanged until the Swift suite, runtime logs, manual runtime evidence, distribution/compliance review, and three uninterrupted passes land. This is how "choose the right weights/KV/neurons/params" becomes software engineering instead of mysticism.

ColdStream residency transport

ColdStream replaces mmap-as-hidden-scheduler with explicit transport. A SemanticWorkingSetPlan emits byte ranges; TransportRunManifest and PageRunScheduler coalesce and prioritize them; SlabArena or MetalBufferLease owns the destination; CodecStage accounts for decompression and copies; TransportTrace reports p95/p99 stalls, read amplification, cancellation, and fallback. SlabArenaCopyCount is now a metadata-only proof that CPU slabs are preallocated, leased, copy-counted, allocation-sampled, rollback-bound, and visible; MetalIOFeatureGate adds the metadata-only proof that Metal is selected only when platform support is explicit and otherwise falls back to CPU slabs; CodecStageLatency adds metadata-only proof that read timing, codec latency, checksums, and copy counts are separate; TransportCancellation adds metadata-only proof that obsolete reads are cancelled and stale slabs are rejected before execution; CachePolicyPollution adds metadata-only proof that cache policy cannot poison repeated hot-route performance behind a hidden transport win; ColdPanicFallback adds metadata-only proof that missed deadlines abort cold wakes, reject stale slabs, queue repair, and surface AnswerPacket caveats; ProductRouteReview adds metadata-only proof that these transport wins do not promote live ColdStream, KV-Direct 128K, dense 70B, or live sparse 70B product routes; SmallModelRuntimeHarnessSafetyPlan adds metadata-only proof that the small-model runtime harness is safe to dry-run first; SmallModelRuntimeHarnessDryRunWitness adds metadata-only proof that runtime-shaped small-model harness evidence can be replayed without runtime/model bytes; SmallModelRuntimeHarnessOwnerApprovedProbe adds metadata-only proof that the first small-model probe is owner-approved, dry-run-bound, local-catalog-bound, cancellable, rollback-bound, AnswerPacket-visible, and execution-deferred; SmallModelRuntimeHarnessAbortableRuntimeProbe adds metadata-only proof that the owner-approved lanes abort before runtime/model bytes open; SmallModelRuntimeHarnessLoggedRuntimeSmoke adds metadata-only proof that missing local snapshots are logged visibly before first-token probing; SmallModelRuntimeHarnessFirstTokenRuntimeProbe, SmallModelRuntimeHarnessAnswerPacketRuntimeProbe, SmallModelRuntimeHarnessProductWrvProbe, SmallModelRuntimeHarnessProductAnswerPacketLiveProbe, SmallModelRuntimeHarnessProductRouteCapabilityRecheck, and SmallModelRuntimeHarnessFreshProductRuntimeSafetyLease now carry the retained-live small-model proof chain through product handoff, blocker recheck, and a metadata-only lease before any fresh product runtime bytes open. The current cursor is small_model_runtime_harness_fresh_product_runtime_l3_release_audit_automated_checks_probe after L3 release-audit zero-fail gate; the fresh live, AnswerPacket, WRV, capability, log-correlation, manual, closeout, and preflight rungs prove the Qwen3-4B product path only as visible L1/L3 evidence while L2 remains red and release readiness remains unclaimed. UAS still names everything, but token-critical cold bytes must be scheduled, not discovered through surprise page faults.

Copy-causal geometry hot-path cure

The mmap replacement layer generalizes ColdStream into a hot-path doctrine. Backend compute, model/KV transport, graph state movement, proof packets, trace summaries, and search artifacts should expose copies, allocations, faults, actor hops, and layout transforms as a CopyCausalGraph. The second-pass lock is Geometry-Aligned Execution: keep mmap as a byte view or baseline, fence it when residency/truncation/cache/copy claims could lie, and replace it only through an explicit read plan, copy budget, layout geometry, proof card, and rollback. The app may still keep intentional copies for multiple graph views, multiple editor surfaces, undo-safe text, previews, snapshots, visual variants, and user artifacts. The cure is targeted: remove hidden compute/transport/proof copies, not useful product state.

PrimitiveWhat it protectsGate
CopyClasscompute-hot, transport-hot, trace-hot, artifact-cold, surface-intentional, undo/correctness-intentional, diagnostic-intentional.F-HotPathCopyScope-IntentionalCopyWaiver

04 · Seven laws + L8-L19 candidates

Every research branch in the corpus passes through these laws. A branch that violates one is preserved as Pro Vault-Preserved speculation, not promoted. A branch that satisfies the seven current laws becomes substrate. Candidate L8 is shown separately because the Erdős synthesis is promising; L9-L19 name newer local-frontier, neural-importance, constructive-residency, cache-lineage, delta-projection, engineering-logic, semantic-working-set, observable-substrate, verifier-calibrated sparse-wake, explicit-residency-transport, and copy-causal-geometry / geometry-aligned-execution bets that still need local falsifiers before canon promotion.

#LawStatement
L1DensityMorph/EML approximates compact controller policies where the formal domain permits.
L2AddressEvery cognitive object has a stable UAS address independent of residency.
L3Active-supportOnly the relevant slice of notes / graph / memory / model / tools / agent state wakes.
L4Lattice-errorEvery compressed or approximate representation pays into WBO.
L5GlueLocal context must cohere before it becomes global context.
L6DuplexHard compact and soft page-backed branches both allowed, but routing error is accounted.
L7WitnessEvery meaningful action is typed, permissioned, logged, replayable, and visible.
L8?Shadow ProjectionCandidate. Every projection from substrate coordinate to user surface preserves source coordinate, pays WBO, and remains reversible up to budget.
L9?Cold Trillion, Hot Five BillionCandidate. A huge addressable atlas may exist cold, but only the smallest verified support set wakes hot.
L10?Counterfactual UtilityCandidate. A neural unit wakes only when expected verifier-improving marginal utility exceeds memory, latency, interference, and rollback cost.
L11?Constructive ResidencyCandidate. Local capability scales with the selected proof-carrying resident assembly, not the largest parameter count simultaneously hot.
L12?Cache LineageCandidate. Persistent cache or trace state promotes only when saved computation and continuity exceed staleness, incompatibility, privacy, storage-wear, and verification cost.
L13?Delta ProjectionCandidate. Every visible note, graph, search, review, or model-context view should update as the smallest verified delta from durable source state.
L14?Engineering LogicCandidate. A mechanism enters architecture only when its invariant, owner, state transition, budget, failure mode, witness, and rollback are explicit.
L15?Semantic Working SetCandidate. A cold cognitive atlas becomes useful only when each mission compiles to a predicted, budgeted, prefetchable, observable working set whose misses update future layout and routing.
L16?Observable SubstrateCandidate. A local cognitive substrate becomes engineerable only when every selected source, page, cache, model route, tool action, proof lane, and failure mode emits a replayable trace frame dense enough for a human or agent to debug.
L17?Verifier-Calibrated Sparse WakeCandidate. A substrate unit wakes only when a small scout predicts verified marginal utility above hot-byte, KV-byte, latency, interference, and rollback cost, and the prediction improves under trace-backed verifier regret.
L18?Explicit Residency TransportCandidate. A cold substrate route becomes token-safe only when predicted cold bytes move through explicit, measured, cancelable transport into leased execution buffers before they can block the hot path.
L19?Copy-Causal GeometryCandidate. A hot path becomes substrate-grade only when its copies, allocations, faults, actor hops, layout transforms, and proof obligations are explicit enough to be reordered, bounded, measured, or waived; mmap is kept as addressability, fenced when it can mislead, and replaced only by measured geometry-aligned execution.
L20?Pattern-Boosted ResidencyCandidate. A cold cognitive atlas becomes practically usable when high-utility resident assemblies are searched, repaired, sparsely fingerprinted, verified, archived, and distilled into reusable route and layout policies before live execution.

05 · The no-orphan invariant

Every data class — every struct, enum, class — must carry five fields, or hold an explicit waiver naming why it is exempt.

FieldTypePurpose
UAS addressUasAddress { kind, payload_id }Stable identity across residency moves
Plane placementRuntimePlane::{State, Episodic, Assembly, Controller, Verification}What the object is for
Build/statusProductBuild::{MAS, Pro} + ProStatus / ResidencyStatusWhere it may ship, what authority band it has, and where it actually lives today vs target
Lattice budgetOption<LatticeBudget>WBO accounting if approximate
WRV status{Wired, Reachable, Visible, Verified}Honest status for product-facing surfaces

Enforced by a clippy::custom_lint rule (Rust side) and a Swift source-guard test mirror. Any new declaration without these comments or an explicit // UAS-EXEMPT: <reason> fails CI.

06 · The five planes

PlaneWhat lives hereExample types

07 · Two builds + Pro tier ladder

Distribution geometry. Epistemos has exactly two distributable builds. MAS ships the safe public floor. Pro contains internal status bands for live, gated, research, vault-preserved, and omega work. Research and Vault are not separate builds.

Two builds

MAS BuildApp Store-safe, conservative, local-first, witnessed, no hidden research runtime.
Pro BuildDirect-distribution power build with internal status bands.

Pro internal ladder

Pro LiveImplemented, visible, tested, and safe for Pro users.
Pro GatedBehind explicit opt-in, rollback, warning, or policy.
Pro ResearchRunnable or document-backed, awaiting falsifier.
Pro Vault-PreservedPreserved ambition with no runtime authority.
Pro OmegaDeepest private experimental substrate work; witnessed and reversible.

Lock phrase: One substrate, two builds, three motions, five planes, visible proof.

Letter grammar

PrefixMeaning
EFoundational theorem or theorem candidate.
HHelios operational claim; translate to a concrete organ before product work.
PCFParameter Connectome Family.
FFalsifier, witness, benchmark, or proof artifact.
WWiring/product-visible work row.
LLaw or invariant.
DDeferred direct-distribution capability.

What is proven vs gated

StatusMeaningPromotion rule
ProvenFormal proof or passing invariant-backed implementation.May be cited as proof only within the proven scope.
EVEvidence-verified by source, log, benchmark, artifact, or measured run.May support product claims only when caller chain and visible surface exist.
EBEngineering bet.Useful direction, not a proof; needs falsifier before promotion.
CandidateAccepted shape awaiting falsifier or product caller proof.Keep named, scoped, and blocked from shipped claims.
GatedBlocked from promotion until a named gate passes.Gate, rollback, and user-visible status are required.
VaultPreservedPreserved ambition, no runtime authority.May guide future work but cannot drive live behavior.
SupersededOld mechanism replaced by a cleaner current organ.Keep historical note; route new work through the current organ.

14 · Falsifier suite (17 + candidate overlays)

Theorems are targets. Falsifiers are the contracts that make a theorem either pass or visibly fail. The substrate ships with the contract written even when the gate isn't yet runnable. 10 falsifier artifact files are now on main after Wave 2; several are schema-normalized primary witnesses and several remain fallback/CPU witnesses that still need Metal/Swift hot-path gates. Erdős lift and copy-causal geometry add candidate overlays that should not count as canon until they have real harnesses.

16 · The math, briefly

E4 / H1
WBO-7 Master Inequality. The end-to-end output of a pipeline of approximating stages is bounded by the sum of per-stage Wyner-Ziv budgets.
‖ŷ − y‖ ≤ Σᵢ βᵢ where βᵢ = WBO budget of stage i
Status: Proven in Lean (E4) and operationally enforced in code (H1). Every LatticeBudget on a cognitive_dag node pays into this ledger.
H4
LatticeCoder / Babai quantization. For a lattice Λ ⊂ ℝⁿ and target vector t, Babai's nearest-plane algorithm gives x ∈ Λ with
‖t − x‖ ≤ (1/2)·√(Σᵢ ‖bᵢ*‖²) (Gram-Schmidt bound)
Status: Proven (classical Babai). Used to bound the quantization stage of E4's pipeline.
H2
Half-softmax post-not-pre rewrite. For attention logits ℓ and post-softmax projection P,
P · softmax(ℓ) = softmax(ℓ + log P) (well-defined when P > 0 elementwise)
Status: Proven; used as the algebraic identity that lets the MAS-equivalent kernel drop-in stay ULP-equivalent.
F-ULP-Oracle
ULP precision bound for fp16 elementary functions on [0.5, 2.0].
∀ x ∈ [0.5, 2.0]: |f_fp16(x) − f_real(x)| / ulp(f_real(x)) ≤ 2 · measured on 412k + 2,048 adversarial points · ≤ 90 s wall clock on M2 Pro
Status: Substrate shipped; harness pending Terminal F.
F-UAS-CopyCount (new)
Zero-copy hot path invariant.
copies(σ) = 0 for σ ∈ {Swift, Rust, Metal, MLX, KV, HNSW}² on the hot inference path
Status: New falsifier defined by Terminal G. PASS = harness binary counts zero copies on a real session.
F-ACS-AnchorLookup (new)
O(1) anchor lookup.
lookup_time(anchor) ≤ 1 μs averaged over 10,000 anchors · M2 Pro 16 GB
Status: New; Terminal G builds the harness against anchor_registry.rs.

The promise. No deferred item ages out of memory. Every deferred item has a build target. Every deferred item has a re-promotion codeword. No deferred item is reclassified to "dropped" without explicit user sign-off. Every LEGENDARY run produces a deferred-work delta report.

18 · Honest boundaries

Does claim

  • Local-first cognition. Any provider route is explicit, user-scoped, and visible.
  • Closed-citation retrieval. An answer may not name a source the retrieval didn't return.
  • Zero-copy address space across Swift, Rust, Metal, MLX, KV, and HNSW on the hot path.
  • Every approximation pays into a Wyner-Ziv error ledger visible in Settings.
  • Every meaningful action is replayable from RunEventLog alone.

Does not (yet) claim

  • Arbitrary live neuron control. Building active-support and circuit-adjacent routing — not direct neuron steering.
  • Pro Research or Pro Omega paths shipping silently in MAS. They stay gated in Pro/private harnesses until promoted by evidence.
  • That MLX is the architecture. MLX is one runtime backend.
  • That deferred work is dropped work — see §17.
